<span class="mw-empty-elt" about="#mwt2" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwAw" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"about","href":"./Template:About"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"the herbicide originally developed by Monsanto"},"2":{"wt":"the active ingredient alone"},"3":{"wt":"glyphosate"}},"i":0}}]}'><style data-mw-deduplicate="TemplateStyles:r1368532237" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles" about="#mwt3" data-mw='{"name":"templatestyles","attrs":{"src":"Module:Hatnote/styles.css"},"body":{"extsrc":""}}'>.mw-parser-output .hatnote{font-style:italic}.mw-parser-output div.hatnote{padding-left:1.6em;margin-bottom:0.5em}.mw-parser-output .hatnote i{font-style:normal}.mw-parser-output .hatnote+.mw-empty-elt+.hatnote{margin-top:-0.5em}@media print{body.ns-0 .mw-parser-output .hatnote{display:none!important}}</style></span><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable" about="#mwt2" id="mwBA">This article is about the herbicide originally developed by Monsanto. For the active ingredient alone, see <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Glyphosate" title="Glyphosate">glyphosate</a>.</div>
<p class="mw-empty-elt" id="mwBQ"><span typeof="mw:Nowiki mw:Transclusion" about="#mwt4" id="mwBg" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"use American English","href":"./Template:Use_American_English"},"params":{"date":{"wt":"August 2022"}},"i":0}}]}'></span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Use_American_English_from_August_2022" about="#mwt4"/><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:All_Wikipedia_articles_written_in_American_English" about="#mwt4" id="mwBw"/>
<span typeof="mw:Nowiki mw:Transclusion" about="#mwt5" id="mwCA" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"use mdy dates","href":"./Template:Use_mdy_dates"},"params":{"date":{"wt":"August 2022"}},"i":0}}]}'></span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Use_mdy_dates_from_August_2022" about="#mwt5" id="mwCQ"/></p>
<span class="mw-empty-elt" about="#mwt6" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwCg"
